# The Ethiopic Didascalia

**Full Title:** The Ethiopic Didascalia; or, The Ethiopic Version of the Apostolical Constitutions, received in the Church of Abyssinia

**Translator:** J.M. Harden, B.D., LL.D.

**Published:** London: Society for Promoting Christian Knowledge, 1920

**Source:** Internet Archive (Cornell University Library scan), [archive.org/details/cu31924096083336](https://archive.org/details/cu31924096083336)

**Public Domain:** Published before 1929 in the United States. No known copyright restrictions.

**Note:** This text was OCR-extracted from the Internet Archive scan and cleaned for readability. Some OCR artifacts may remain. Footnotes and manuscript variant annotations from the original have been removed for readability. Bracketed references like [AC ii., 5] indicate the corresponding chapter in the Apostolic Constitutions (Funk's numeration). For scholarly footnotes and manuscript variants, consult the original scan.

---
